### 2. `recipientId` exposure in `CreateShareResponseDto`

**NOT ADDRESSED.** The DTO at `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/api/src/shares/dto/share-response.dto.ts` still exposes `recipientId` (line 8):

```typescript
export class CreateShareResponseDto {
  @ApiProperty({ description: 'UUID of the created share' })
  shareId!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ description: 'UUID of the recipient user' })
  recipientId!: string;   // <-- still exposes internal UUID
```

And the controller at line 71 still returns it:

```typescript
return {
  shareId: share.id,
  recipientId: share.recipientId,   // <-- internal UUID still returned
  ...
};
```

---

### 3. `publicKey` query param validation on lookup endpoint

**NOT ADDRESSED.** The controller at `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/api/src/shares/shares.controller.ts` line 155 accepts the raw string without any format validation:

```typescript
async lookupUser(@Query('publicKey') publicKey: string): Promise<{ exists: boolean }> {
    const exists = await this.sharesService.lookupUserByPublicKey(publicKey);
```

There is no `@Matches()` decorator, no `ValidationPipe` on this parameter, and no regex/format check. The client-side `ShareDialog.tsx` does have `isValidPublicKey()` validation, but the server endpoint itself has none.

---

### 4. Missing `@ApiResponse({ status: 403 })` on `completeRotation`

**NOT ADDRESSED.** At `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/api/src/shares/shares.controller.ts` lines 297-313, the `completeRotation` method only has:

```typescript
@ApiResponse({ status: 204, description: 'Share hard-deleted after rotation' })
@ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
@ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Share not found' })
```

There is no `@ApiResponse({ status: 403 })` even though the service throws `ForbiddenException` at line 281:

```typescript
if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
  throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can complete rotation');
}
```

---

### 5. `completeRotation` guard -- hard-deletes active (non-revoked) shares

**NOT ADDRESSED.** At `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/api/src/shares/shares.service.ts` lines 273-286, `completeRotation` does not check `revokedAt`:

```typescript
async completeRotation(shareId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });

    if (!share) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
    }

    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can complete rotation');
    }

    // CASCADE will remove all associated ShareKey records
    await this.shareRepo.remove(share);   // <-- no revokedAt check
  }
```

An active (non-revoked) share can be hard-deleted by calling this endpoint. There should be a guard like:

```typescript
if (!share.revokedAt) {
  throw new ConflictException('Cannot complete rotation for a non-revoked share');
}
```

---

### 6. Key zeroing -- `reWrapEncryptedKey` plainKey not zeroed if `wrapKey` throws

**ADDRESSED.** At `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/ShareDialog.tsx` lines 166-175, `plainKey.fill(0)` is called unconditionally after `wrapKey`:

```typescript
async function reWrapEncryptedKey(
  encryptedKeyHex: string,
  ownerPrivateKey: Uint8Array,
  recipientPubKey: Uint8Array
): Promise<string> {
  const plainKey = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(encryptedKeyHex), ownerPrivateKey);
  const rewrapped = await wrapKey(plainKey, recipientPubKey);
  plainKey.fill(0);
  return bytesToHex(rewrapped);
}
```

However, this is still **NOT ADDRESSED** in the strict sense -- if `wrapKey(plainKey, recipientPubKey)` throws, the `plainKey.fill(0)` on line 173 is never reached. A `try/finally` is needed:

```typescript
const plainKey = await unwrapKey(...);
try {
  const rewrapped = await wrapKey(plainKey, recipientPubKey);
  return bytesToHex(rewrapped);
} finally {
  plainKey.fill(0);
}
```

**NOT ADDRESSED** -- `plainKey` is not zeroed if `wrapKey` throws.

---

### 7. Key zeroing -- `itemFolderKey` not zeroed when intermediate `await` throws

**PARTIALLY ADDRESSED.** At `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/components/file-browser/ShareDialog.tsx` lines 331-362, `itemFolderKey` is allocated at line 331 and zeroed at line 362:

```typescript
const itemFolderKey = await unwrapKey(
  hexToBytes(folderEntry.folderKeyEncrypted),
  ownerPrivateKey
);

// ... several awaits in between (lines 337-358) that could throw ...

// Zero the folder key
itemFolderKey.fill(0);
```

The zeroing at line 362 is outside any `try/finally`. If `wrapKey` (line 337), `resolveIpnsRecord` (line 342), `fetchFromIpfs` (line 344), `decryptFolderMetadata` (line 347), or `collectChildKeys` (line 352) throws, `itemFolderKey` is never zeroed. The outer `catch` at line 414 does not zero it either.

**NOT ADDRESSED** -- `itemFolderKey` needs a `try/finally` wrapper.

---

### 8. Key zeroing -- `useSharedNavigation` unmount cleanup

**PARTIALLY ADDRESSED.** The hook at `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useSharedNavigation.ts` does zero keys in `navigateToRoot` (lines 320-333) and `navigateUp` (line 304). However, there is **no cleanup on component unmount**. The `useEffect` on lines 113-148 returns a cleanup that only sets `cancelled = true` -- it does not zero `folderKey` or nav stack keys:

```typescript
useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    async function loadShares() { ... }
    loadShares();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;   // <-- no key zeroing here
    };
  }, []);
```

If the component unmounts without the user explicitly navigating to root, decrypted folder keys in `folderKey` state and `navStackRef.current[*].folderKey` remain in memory.

**NOT ADDRESSED** -- no unmount cleanup for decrypted keys.

**NOT ADDRESSED.** At `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useSharedNavigation.ts` lines 188-215:

```typescript
const itemKey = await unwrapKey(
  hexToBytes(share.encryptedKey),
  auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey
);

if (share.itemType === 'folder') {
  // ... itemKey is kept as folderKey state (which is later zeroed on navigation)
  setFolderKey(itemKey);
  ...
} else {
  // For files, trigger download directly
  await downloadSharedFileFromShare(share, auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey);
  // itemKey is NEVER zeroed in this branch
}
```

In the `else` branch (file-type shares), `itemKey` is unwrapped but never zeroed. The `downloadSharedFileFromShare` function uses the share's `encryptedKey` independently (it unwraps its own `parentFolderKey`), so `itemKey` just leaks.

**NOT ADDRESSED.**

---

### 10. Key zeroing -- `parentFolderKey` not zeroed in `finally` block

**ADDRESSED.** At `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useSharedNavigation.ts` lines 353-365, `parentFolderKey` is zeroed:

```typescript
const parentFolderKey = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(share.encryptedKey), privateKey);

// ... usage ...
const fileMeta = await decryptFileMetadata(encrypted, parentFolderKey);
parentFolderKey.fill(0);   // line 365
```

However, this zeroing is **not** in a `finally` block. If any of the intermediate `await` calls (lines 356-364) throw, `parentFolderKey` is not zeroed -- the `catch` at line 389 does not zero it.

**NOT ADDRESSED** -- `parentFolderKey.fill(0)` at line 365 is not inside a `try/finally`, so it's skipped on error.

---

### 11. Key zeroing -- `newFolderKey` not zeroed before throwing on partial re-wrap failure

**NOT ADDRESSED.** At `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/share.service.ts` lines 392-431:

```typescript
const newFolderKey = generateRandomBytes(32);

// ... re-wrap for recipients ...

if (reWrapFailures.length > 0) {
    throw new Error(
      `Key rotation failed: could not re-wrap key for ${reWrapFailures.length} recipient(s). ` +
        'Aborting to prevent inconsistent state.'
    );
    // newFolderKey is NOT zeroed before this throw
  }
```

When re-wrapping fails for some recipients, `newFolderKey` (a 32-byte plaintext AES key) is abandoned in memory without zeroing.

**NOT ADDRESSED.**

---

### 12. IPNS sequence number -- should use `resolved.sequenceNumber`

**NOT ADDRESSED.** At `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/folder.service.ts` lines 998-1022:

```typescript
const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderNode.ipnsName);
// resolved has .sequenceNumber (fresh from IPNS resolution)

const currentMetadata = await fetchAndDecryptMetadata(resolved.cid, folderNode.folderKey);

await updateFolderMetadata({
    folderId: folderNode.id,
    children: currentMetadata.children ?? [],
    folderKey: newFolderKey,
    ipnsPrivateKey: folderNode.ipnsPrivateKey,
    ipnsName: folderNode.ipnsName,
    sequenceNumber: folderNode.sequenceNumber,   // <-- uses STALE value from store
  });
```

The `resolveIpnsRecord` call on line 998 returns `{ cid, sequenceNumber, signatureVerified }` with the fresh `sequenceNumber`. But line 1021 uses `folderNode.sequenceNumber` (the cached value from the folder store), which may be stale if another device or the TEE republished in the meantime. The correct code should be `sequenceNumber: resolved.sequenceNumber`.

**NOT ADDRESSED.**

All 6 test coverage threads resolved. Here's the summary:

**Resolved (replied + marked resolved): 6 threads**
- All 6 CI/test coverage threads — tests were added in commit `68ba6a5`

**Already resolved (9 threads)** — no action needed

**Still unresolved — 11 new comments that need code fixes:**

### API Issues (4 threads)
1. **`recipientId` exposure** — `CreateShareResponseDto` leaks internal UUIDs
2. **`publicKey` query param** — no format validation on lookup endpoint
3. **Missing `@ApiResponse(403)`** — on `completeRotation` endpoint
4. **`completeRotation` guard** — allows hard-deleting active (non-revoked) shares without checking `revokedAt`

### Key Zeroing / Memory Safety (6 threads)
5. **`reWrapEncryptedKey`** — `plainKey` not zeroed if `wrapKey` throws (needs try/finally)
6. **`itemFolderKey`** in ShareDialog — not zeroed when intermediate `await` throws
7. **`useSharedNavigation` unmount** — decrypted folder keys not zeroed on component unmount
8. **`itemKey`** — never zeroed for file-type shares in `useSharedNavigation`
9. **`parentFolderKey`** — not zeroed in `finally` block in `useSharedNavigation`
10. **`newFolderKey`** — not zeroed before throwing on partial re-wrap failure in `share.service.ts`

### Data Integrity (1 thread)
11. **IPNS sequence number** — `folder.service.ts` uses stale `folderNode.sequenceNumber` instead of fresh `resolved.sequenceNumber`

Would you like me to fix these 11 remaining issues?

     1→import {
     2→  Injectable,
     3→  NotFoundException,
     4→  ForbiddenException,
     5→  ConflictException,
     6→} from '@nestjs/common';
     7→import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
     8→import { Repository, IsNull, Not } from 'typeorm';
     9→import { Share } from './entities/share.entity';
    10→import { ShareKey } from './entities/share-key.entity';
    11→import { User } from '../auth/entities/user.entity';
    12→import { CreateShareDto } from './dto/create-share.dto';
    13→import { AddShareKeysDto } from './dto/share-key.dto';
    14→
    15→@Injectable()
    16→export class SharesService {
    17→  constructor(
    18→    @InjectRepository(Share)
    19→    private readonly shareRepo: Repository<Share>,
    20→    @InjectRepository(ShareKey)
    21→    private readonly shareKeyRepo: Repository<ShareKey>,
    22→    @InjectRepository(User)
    23→    private readonly userRepo: Repository<User>
    24→  ) {}
    25→
    26→  /**
    27→   * Create a new share record with re-wrapped keys.
    28→   * Validates recipient exists and is not the sharer.
    29→   * Prevents duplicate active shares for the same item/recipient pair.
    30→   */
    31→  async createShare(sharerId: string, dto: CreateShareDto): Promise<Share> {
    32→    // Look up recipient by publicKey
    33→    const recipient = await this.userRepo.findOne({
    34→      where: { publicKey: dto.recipientPublicKey },
    35→    });
    36→
    37→    if (!recipient) {
    38→      throw new NotFoundException('Recipient not found');
    39→    }
    40→
    41→    if (recipient.id === sharerId) {
    42→      throw new ConflictException('Cannot share with yourself');
    43→    }
    44→
    45→    // Check for existing active share (same sharer, recipient, ipnsName)
    46→    const existing = await this.shareRepo.findOne({
    47→      where: {
    48→        sharerId,
    49→        recipientId: recipient.id,
    50→        ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    51→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
    52→      },
    53→    });
    54→
    55→    if (existing) {
    56→      throw new ConflictException('Share already exists for this item and recipient');
    57→    }
    58→
    59→    // Clean up any revoked-but-not-yet-rotated records for this triple
    60→    // so the new share can be created without unique constraint conflicts
    61→    const revoked = await this.shareRepo.find({
    62→      where: {
    63→        sharerId,
    64→        recipientId: recipient.id,
    65→        ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    66→        revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),
    67→      },
    68→    });
    69→    if (revoked.length > 0) {
    70→      await this.shareRepo.remove(revoked);
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    const share = this.shareRepo.create({
    74→      sharerId,
    75→      recipientId: recipient.id,
    76→      itemType: dto.itemType,
    77→      ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    78→      itemName: dto.itemName,
    79→      encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
    80→      hiddenByRecipient: false,
    81→      revokedAt: null,
    82→    });
    83→
    84→    let savedShare: typeof share;
    85→    try {
    86→      savedShare = await this.shareRepo.save(share);
    87→    } catch (err: unknown) {
    88→      // Handle race condition: concurrent createShare for the same triple
    89→      if (err instanceof Error && err.message?.includes('duplicate key')) {
    90→        throw new ConflictException('Share already exists for this item and recipient');
    91→      }
    92→      throw err;
    93→    }
    94→
    95→    // Create child keys if provided
    96→    if (dto.childKeys && dto.childKeys.length > 0) {
    97→      const shareKeys = dto.childKeys.map((ck) =>
    98→        this.shareKeyRepo.create({
    99→          shareId: savedShare.id,
   100→          keyType: ck.keyType,
   101→          itemId: ck.itemId,
   102→          encryptedKey: Buffer.from(ck.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   103→        })
   104→      );
   105→      await this.shareKeyRepo.save(shareKeys);
   106→    }
   107→
   108→    return savedShare;
   109→  }
   110→
   111→  /**
   112→   * Get all active, non-hidden shares received by the user.
   113→   * Includes sharer relation for publicKey display.
   114→   */
   115→  async getReceivedShares(recipientId: string): Promise<Share[]> {
   116→    return this.shareRepo.find({
   117→      where: {
   118→        recipientId,
   119→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
   120→        hiddenByRecipient: false,
   121→      },
   122→      relations: ['sharer'],
   123→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
   124→    });
   125→  }
   126→
   127→  /**
   128→   * Get all active shares sent by the user.
   129→   * Includes recipient relation for publicKey display.
   130→   */
   131→  async getSentShares(sharerId: string): Promise<Share[]> {
   132→    return this.shareRepo.find({
   133→      where: {
   134→        sharerId,
   135→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
   136→      },
   137→      relations: ['recipient'],
   138→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
   139→    });
   140→  }
   141→
   142→  /**
   143→   * Get all re-wrapped child keys for a share.
   144→   * Validates the requesting user is either sharer or recipient.
   145→   */
   146→  async getShareKeys(shareId: string, userId: string): Promise<ShareKey[]> {
   147→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   148→
   149→    if (!share) {
   150→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   151→    }
   152→
   153→    if (share.sharerId !== userId && share.recipientId !== userId) {
   154→      throw new ForbiddenException('Not authorized to access this share');
   155→    }
   156→
   157→    return this.shareKeyRepo.find({
   158→      where: { shareId },
   159→      order: { createdAt: 'ASC' },
   160→    });
   161→  }
   162→
   163→  /**
   164→   * Add or update re-wrapped keys for an existing share.
   165→   * Only the sharer can add keys.
   166→   */
   167→  async addShareKeys(shareId: string, sharerId: string, dto: AddShareKeysDto): Promise<void> {
   168→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   169→
   170→    if (!share) {
   171→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   175→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can add keys');
   176→    }
   177→
   178→    // Upsert: insert or update encrypted_key for each itemId
   179→    for (const entry of dto.keys) {
   180→      const existing = await this.shareKeyRepo.findOne({
   181→        where: {
   182→          shareId,
   183→          keyType: entry.keyType,
   184→          itemId: entry.itemId,
   185→        },
   186→      });
   187→
   188→      if (existing) {
   189→        existing.encryptedKey = Buffer.from(entry.encryptedKey, 'hex');
   190→        await this.shareKeyRepo.save(existing);
   191→      } else {
   192→        const shareKey = this.shareKeyRepo.create({
   193→          shareId,
   194→          keyType: entry.keyType,
   195→          itemId: entry.itemId,
   196→          encryptedKey: Buffer.from(entry.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   197→        });
   198→        await this.shareKeyRepo.save(shareKey);
   199→      }
   200→    }
   201→  }
   202→
   203→  /**
   204→   * Soft-delete a share by setting revokedAt.
   205→   * Only the sharer can revoke. ShareKey records are kept for lazy rotation.
   206→   */
   207→  async revokeShare(shareId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
   208→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   209→
   210→    if (!share) {
   211→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   212→    }
   213→
   214→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   215→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can revoke a share');
   216→    }
   217→
   218→    share.revokedAt = new Date();
   219→    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   220→  }
   221→
   222→  /**
   223→   * Hide a share from the recipient's view.
   224→   * Only the recipient can hide a share.
   225→   */
   226→  async hideShare(shareId: string, recipientId: string): Promise<void> {
   227→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   228→
   229→    if (!share) {
   230→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   231→    }
   232→
   233→    if (share.recipientId !== recipientId) {
   234→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the recipient can hide a share');
   235→    }
   236→
   237→    share.hiddenByRecipient = true;
   238→    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   239→  }
   240→
   241→  /**
   242→   * Check if a user with the given secp256k1 public key exists.
   243→   * Used to verify recipient is registered before sharing.
   244→   * Does not expose internal user IDs.
   245→   */
   246→  async lookupUserByPublicKey(publicKey: string): Promise<boolean> {
   247→    const user = await this.userRepo.findOne({
   248→      where: { publicKey },
   249→      select: ['id'],
   250→    });
   251→
   252→    return !!user;
   253→  }
   254→
   255→  /**
   256→   * Get shares pending key rotation (revoked but not yet hard-deleted).
   257→   */
   258→  async getPendingRotations(sharerId: string): Promise<Share[]> {
   259→    return this.shareRepo.find({
   260→      where: {
   261→        sharerId,
   262→        revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),
   263→      },
   264→      relations: ['recipient'],
   265→      order: { revokedAt: 'ASC' },
   266→    });
   267→  }
   268→
   269→  /**
   270→   * Hard-delete a share and all associated keys after rotation is complete.
   271→   * Only the sharer can complete the rotation.
   272→   */
   273→  async completeRotation(shareId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
   274→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   275→
   276→    if (!share) {
   277→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   278→    }
   279→
   280→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   281→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can complete rotation');
   282→    }
   283→
   284→    // CASCADE will remove all associated ShareKey records
   285→    await this.shareRepo.remove(share);
   286→  }
   287→
   288→  /**
   289→   * Update the encrypted key on an existing share.
   290→   * Used after lazy key rotation to re-wrap the new folder key for remaining recipients.
   291→   */
   292→  async updateShareEncryptedKey(
   293→    shareId: string,
   294→    sharerId: string,
   295→    encryptedKey: string
   296→  ): Promise<void> {
   297→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   298→
   299→    if (!share) {
   300→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   301→    }
   302→
   303→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   304→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can update share keys');
   305→    }
   306→
   307→    share.encryptedKey = Buffer.from(encryptedKey, 'hex');
   308→    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   309→  }
   310→}

     1→import { useState, useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import type { FolderChild, FolderEntry, FilePointer } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
     3→import {
     4→  wrapKey,
     5→  unwrapKey,
     6→  hexToBytes,
     7→  bytesToHex,
     8→  decryptFolderMetadata,
     9→} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    10→import { Modal } from '../ui/Modal';
    11→import { useAuthStore } from '../../stores/auth.store';
    12→import { useFolderStore } from '../../stores/folder.store';
    13→import {
    14→  sharesControllerCreateShare,
    15→  sharesControllerLookupUser,
    16→  sharesControllerGetSentShares,
    17→  sharesControllerRevokeShare,
    18→} from '../../api/shares/shares';
    19→import { resolveFileMetadata } from '../../services/file-metadata.service';
    20→import { resolveIpnsRecord } from '../../services/ipns.service';
    21→import { fetchFromIpfs } from '../../lib/api/ipfs';
    22→import type { CreateShareDtoItemType } from '../../api/models/createShareDtoItemType';
    23→import type { ChildKeyDto } from '../../api/models/childKeyDto';
    24→import '../../styles/share-dialog.css';
    25→
    26→type ShareDialogProps = {
    27→  isOpen: boolean;
    28→  onClose: () => void;
    29→  item: FolderChild;
    30→  folderKey: Uint8Array;
    31→  ipnsName: string;
    32→  parentFolderId: string;
    33→};
    34→
    35→/** Sent share record from the API */
    36→type SentShare = {
    37→  shareId: string;
    38→  recipientPublicKey: string;
    39→  itemType: string;
    40→  ipnsName: string;
    41→  itemName: string;
    42→  createdAt: string;
    43→};
    44→
    45→/**
    46→ * Validate public key format: must be 0x04 prefix + 128 hex chars (64 bytes body = 65 bytes total uncompressed).
    47→ */
    48→function isValidPublicKey(key: string): boolean {
    49→  if (!key.startsWith('0x04')) return false;
    50→  // 0x + 130 hex chars = 65 bytes uncompressed secp256k1
    51→  const hexPart = key.slice(2);
    52→  if (hexPart.length !== 130) return false;
    53→  return /^[0-9a-fA-F]+$/.test(hexPart);
    54→}
    55→
    56→/**
    57→ * Truncate a public key for display: 0x{first4}...{last4}
    58→ */
    59→function truncateKey(key: string): string {
    60→  if (key.length < 12) return key;
    61→  const hex = key.startsWith('0x') ? key.slice(2) : key;
    62→  return `0x${hex.slice(0, 4)}...${hex.slice(-4)}`;
    63→}
    64→
    65→/**
    66→ * Collect all descendant keys from a folder for sharing.
    67→ * Traverses subfolders depth-first, re-wrapping each file and subfolder key
    68→ * for the recipient.
    69→ *
    70→ * @param children - Children of the current folder
    71→ * @param folderKey - Decrypted AES key of the current folder (for resolving file metadata)
    72→ * @param ownerPrivateKey - Owner's secp256k1 private key for unwrapping ECIES keys
    73→ * @param recipientPubKeyBytes - Recipient's uncompressed secp256k1 public key
    74→ * @param onProgress - Callback for progress tracking
    75→ */
    76→async function collectChildKeys(
    77→  children: FolderChild[],
    78→  folderKey: Uint8Array,
    79→  ownerPrivateKey: Uint8Array,
    80→  recipientPubKeyBytes: Uint8Array,
    81→  onProgress: (wrapped: number) => void
    82→): Promise<ChildKeyDto[]> {
    83→  const childKeys: ChildKeyDto[] = [];
    84→  let wrapped = 0;
    85→
    86→  for (const child of children) {
    87→    if (child.type === 'file') {
    88→      const fp = child as FilePointer;
    89→      // Resolve file metadata to get fileKeyEncrypted, then re-wrap for recipient
    90→      try {
    91→        const { metadata: fileMeta } = await resolveFileMetadata(fp.fileMetaIpnsName, folderKey);
    92→        const reWrappedFileKey = await reWrapEncryptedKey(
    93→          fileMeta.fileKeyEncrypted,
    94→          ownerPrivateKey,
    95→          recipientPubKeyBytes
    96→        );
    97→        childKeys.push({
    98→          keyType: 'file' as ChildKeyDto['keyType'],
    99→          itemId: fp.id,
   100→          encryptedKey: reWrappedFileKey,
   101→        });
   102→        wrapped++;
   103→        onProgress(wrapped);
   104→      } catch (err) {
   105→        console.error(`Failed to re-wrap file key for ${fp.name}:`, err);
   106→        // Continue with other children
   107→      }
   108→    } else {
   109→      const folder = child as FolderEntry;
   110→      // Re-wrap the subfolder's folderKey for the recipient
   111→      const folderKeyRewrapped = await reWrapEncryptedKey(
   112→        folder.folderKeyEncrypted,
   113→        ownerPrivateKey,
   114→        recipientPubKeyBytes
   115→      );
   116→      childKeys.push({
   117→        keyType: 'folder' as ChildKeyDto['keyType'],
   118→        itemId: folder.id,
   119→        encryptedKey: folderKeyRewrapped,
   120→      });
   121→      wrapped++;
   122→      onProgress(wrapped);
   123→
   124→      // Recurse into subfolder: resolve its metadata and collect its children
   125→      try {
   126→        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folder.ipnsName);
   127→        if (resolved) {
   128→          const folderKeyBytes = await unwrapKey(
   129→            hexToBytes(folder.folderKeyEncrypted),
   130→            ownerPrivateKey
   131→          );
   132→          try {
   133→            const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
   134→            const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
   135→            const encrypted = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
   136→            const metadata = await decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, folderKeyBytes);
   137→
   138→            const subKeys = await collectChildKeys(
   139→              metadata.children,
   140→              folderKeyBytes,
   141→              ownerPrivateKey,
   142→              recipientPubKeyBytes,
   143→              (subWrapped) => {
   144→                onProgress(wrapped + subWrapped);
   145→              }
   146→            );
   147→            wrapped += subKeys.length;
   148→            childKeys.push(...subKeys);
   149→          } finally {
   150→            folderKeyBytes.fill(0);
   151→          }
   152→        }
   153→      } catch (err) {
   154→        console.error(`Failed to traverse subfolder ${folder.name}:`, err);
   155→        // Continue with other children
   156→      }
   157→    }
   158→  }
   159→
   160→  return childKeys;
   161→}
   162→
   163→/**
   164→ * Re-wrap an ECIES-encrypted hex key from owner to recipient.
   165→ */
   166→async function reWrapEncryptedKey(
   167→  encryptedKeyHex: string,
   168→  ownerPrivateKey: Uint8Array,
   169→  recipientPubKey: Uint8Array
   170→): Promise<string> {
   171→  const plainKey = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(encryptedKeyHex), ownerPrivateKey);
   172→  const rewrapped = await wrapKey(plainKey, recipientPubKey);
   173→  plainKey.fill(0);
   174→  return bytesToHex(rewrapped);
   175→}
   176→
   177→/**
   178→ * Count total items in a folder for progress tracking.
   179→ * Includes both files and subfolders since all need key re-wrapping.
   180→ */
   181→function countFolderChildren(children: FolderChild[]): number {
   182→  return children.length;
   183→}
   184→
   185→/**
   186→ * Share dialog modal for creating and managing shares.
   187→ *
   188→ * Allows users to:
   189→ * - Paste a recipient's public key and create a share
   190→ * - View existing recipients with truncated pubkeys
   191→ * - Revoke individual recipients with inline confirm
   192→ *
   193→ * For folders, traverses all descendants and re-wraps keys.
   194→ * For files, wraps the single file key for the recipient.
   195→ */
   196→export function ShareDialog({
   197→  isOpen,
   198→  onClose,
   199→  item,
   200→  folderKey,
   201→  ipnsName,
   202→  parentFolderId,
   203→}: ShareDialogProps) {
   204→  const [pubKeyInput, setPubKeyInput] = useState('');
   205→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   206→  const [success, setSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);
   207→  const [isSharing, setIsSharing] = useState(false);
   208→  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<{ current: number; total: number } | null>(null);
   209→  const [recipients, setRecipients] = useState<SentShare[]>([]);
   210→  const [recipientsLoading, setRecipientsLoading] = useState(false);
   211→  const [confirmRevokeId, setConfirmRevokeId] = useState<string | null>(null);
   212→  const [revokingId, setRevokingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
   213→
   214→  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   215→
   216→  // Fetch existing recipients when dialog opens
   217→  useEffect(() => {
   218→    if (!isOpen) {
   219→      // Reset state on close
   220→      setPubKeyInput('');
   221→      setError(null);
   222→      setSuccess(null);
   223→      setIsSharing(false);
   224→      setProgress(null);
   225→      setConfirmRevokeId(null);
   226→      setRevokingId(null);
   227→      return;
   228→    }
   229→
   230→    let cancelled = false;
   231→    setRecipientsLoading(true);
   232→
   233→    sharesControllerGetSentShares()
   234→      .then((shares) => {
   235→        if (cancelled) return;
   236→        // Filter to shares for this specific item (by ipnsName)
   237→        const itemShares: SentShare[] = shares
   238→          .filter((s) => s.ipnsName === ipnsName)
   239→          .map((s) => ({
   240→            shareId: s.shareId,
   241→            recipientPublicKey: s.recipientPublicKey,
   242→            itemType: s.itemType as 'folder' | 'file',
   243→            ipnsName: s.ipnsName,
   244→            itemName: s.itemName,
   245→            createdAt: String(s.createdAt),
   246→          }));
   247→        setRecipients(itemShares);
   248→      })
   249→      .catch((err) => {
   250→        if (cancelled) return;
   251→        console.error('Failed to fetch sent shares:', err);
   252→      })
   253→      .finally(() => {
   254→        if (!cancelled) setRecipientsLoading(false);
   255→      });
   256→
   257→    return () => {
   258→      cancelled = true;
   259→    };
   260→  }, [isOpen, ipnsName]);
   261→
   262→  const handleShare = useCallback(async () => {
   263→    setError(null);
   264→    setSuccess(null);
   265→
   266→    const key = pubKeyInput.trim();
   267→
   268→    // Validate format
   269→    if (!isValidPublicKey(key)) {
   270→      setError('invalid key format -- expected 0x04 + 128 hex chars');
   271→      return;
   272→    }
   273→
   274→    // Prevent sharing root folder
   275→    if (parentFolderId === 'root' && item.type === 'folder') {
   276→      // Check if item is the root folder itself (the one navigated to at root)
   277→      const rootFolder = useFolderStore.getState().folders['root'];
   278→      if (rootFolder && item.id === 'root') {
   279→        setError('cannot share root folder');
   280→        return;
   281→      }
   282→    }
   283→
   284→    // Check not sharing with yourself
   285→    const vaultKeypair = useAuthStore.getState().vaultKeypair;
   286→    if (!vaultKeypair) {
   287→      setError('vault keypair not available');
   288→      return;
   289→    }
   290→
   291→    const myPubKeyHex = '0x' + bytesToHex(vaultKeypair.publicKey);
   292→    if (key.toLowerCase() === myPubKeyHex.toLowerCase()) {
   293→      setError('cannot share with yourself');
   294→      return;
   295→    }
   296→
   297→    setIsSharing(true);
   298→
   299→    try {
   300→      // Verify recipient is a registered user
   301→      await sharesControllerLookupUser({ publicKey: key });
   302→    } catch {
   303→      setError('user not found');
   304→      setIsSharing(false);
   305→      return;
   306→    }
   307→
   308→    try {
   309→      const recipientPubKeyBytes = hexToBytes(key.slice(2));
   310→      const ownerPrivateKey = vaultKeypair.privateKey;
   311→
   312→      let encryptedKey: string;
   313→      let childKeys: ChildKeyDto[] | undefined;
   314→
   315→      if (item.type === 'folder') {
   316→        const folderEntry = item as FolderEntry;
   317→
   318→        // For folders shared from a parent, folderKey IS the folder's own key
   319→        // (it's the folderKey passed as prop which is the parent's key --
   320→        //  but the item is a FolderEntry child that has folderKeyEncrypted)
   321→        // We need to use folderKeyEncrypted from the FolderEntry to get the actual folder key.
   322→        // Wait -- the folderKey prop is the PARENT folder's key. The item's own key
   323→        // is obtained by unwrapping folderKeyEncrypted from the FolderEntry.
   324→        // Actually, looking at the plan more carefully:
   325→        //   "For folders: folderKey is passed as prop"
   326→        // But folderKey is the PARENT's key. For sharing a subfolder, we need the subfolder's key.
   327→        // Let's check: if the item is a direct child folder, its folderKeyEncrypted
   328→        // is wrapped with the owner's public key (ECIES). We unwrap it to get the folder's own key.
   329→
   330→        // Unwrap the folder's own key from its encrypted form
   331→        const itemFolderKey = await unwrapKey(
   332→          hexToBytes(folderEntry.folderKeyEncrypted),
   333→          ownerPrivateKey
   334→        );
   335→
   336→        // Wrap the folder key for the recipient
   337→        const wrappedForRecipient = await wrapKey(itemFolderKey, recipientPubKeyBytes);
   338→        encryptedKey = bytesToHex(wrappedForRecipient);
   339→
   340→        // Now traverse children and re-wrap descendant keys
   341→        // First, resolve folder metadata to get children
   342→        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderEntry.ipnsName);
   343→        if (resolved) {
   344→          const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
   345→          const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
   346→          const encrypted = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
   347→          const metadata = await decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, itemFolderKey);
   348→
   349→          const totalSubfolders = countFolderChildren(metadata.children);
   350→          setProgress({ current: 0, total: totalSubfolders });
   351→
   352→          childKeys = await collectChildKeys(
   353→            metadata.children,
   354→            itemFolderKey,
   355→            ownerPrivateKey,
   356→            recipientPubKeyBytes,
   357→            (wrapped) => setProgress({ current: wrapped, total: totalSubfolders })
   358→          );
   359→        }
   360→
   361→        // Zero the folder key
   362→        itemFolderKey.fill(0);
   363→      } else {
   364→        // File sharing: wrap parent folder key for recipient (needed to decrypt file metadata),
   365→        // and re-wrap the file key as a child key entry
   366→        const filePointer = item as FilePointer;
   367→
   368→        // encryptedKey = parent folder key wrapped for recipient
   369→        const wrappedFolderKey = await wrapKey(folderKey, recipientPubKeyBytes);
   370→        encryptedKey = bytesToHex(wrappedFolderKey);
   371→
   372→        // Re-wrap the file key for the recipient and store as child key
   373→        const { metadata: fileMeta } = await resolveFileMetadata(
   374→          filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
   375→          folderKey
   376→        );
   377→        const reWrappedFileKey = await reWrapEncryptedKey(
   378→          fileMeta.fileKeyEncrypted,
   379→          ownerPrivateKey,
   380→          recipientPubKeyBytes
   381→        );
   382→        childKeys = [
   383→          { keyType: 'file' as const, itemId: filePointer.id, encryptedKey: reWrappedFileKey },
   384→        ];
   385→      }
   386→
   387→      // Create the share via API
   388→      const itemType: CreateShareDtoItemType = item.type === 'folder' ? 'folder' : 'file';
   389→      const result = await sharesControllerCreateShare({
   390→        recipientPublicKey: key,
   391→        itemType,
   392→        ipnsName,
   393→        itemName: item.name,
   394→        encryptedKey,
   395→        childKeys: childKeys && childKeys.length > 0 ? childKeys : undefined,
   396→      });
   397→
   398→      // Update recipients list
   399→      setRecipients((prev) => [
   400→        ...prev,
   401→        {
   402→          shareId: result.shareId,
   403→          recipientPublicKey: key,
   404→          itemType: item.type,
   405→          ipnsName,
   406→          itemName: item.name,
   407→          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   408→        },
   409→      ]);
   410→
   411→      setSuccess(`shared with ${truncateKey(key)}`);
   412→      setPubKeyInput('');
   413→      setProgress(null);
   414→    } catch (err) {
   415→      console.error('Share creation failed:', err);
   416→      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'share creation failed';
   417→      setError(message);
   418→    } finally {
   419→      setIsSharing(false);
   420→      setProgress(null);
   421→    }
   422→  }, [pubKeyInput, item, folderKey, ipnsName, parentFolderId]);
   423→
   424→  const handleRevoke = useCallback(async (shareId: string) => {
   425→    setRevokingId(shareId);
   426→    setConfirmRevokeId(null);
   427→    try {
   428→      await sharesControllerRevokeShare(shareId);
   429→      setRecipients((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.shareId !== shareId));
   430→    } catch (err) {
   431→      console.error('Revoke failed:', err);
   432→      setError('revoke failed');
   433→    } finally {
   434→      setRevokingId(null);
   435→    }
   436→  }, []);
   437→
   438→  const handleKeyDown = useCallback(
   439→    (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
   440→      if (e.key === 'Enter' && !isSharing) {
   441→        handleShare();
   442→      }
   443→    },
   444→    [handleShare, isSharing]
   445→  );
   446→
   447→  const itemDisplayName = item.type === 'folder' ? `${item.name}/` : item.name;
   448→  const title = `SHARE: ${itemDisplayName}`;
   449→
   450→  return (
   451→    <Modal open={isOpen} onClose={onClose} title={title}>
   452→      <div className="share-dialog">
   453→        {/* Input section */}
   454→        <div className="share-input-section">
   455→          <label className="share-input-label" htmlFor="share-pubkey-input">
   456→            {'// paste recipient public key'}
   457→          </label>
   458→          <div className="share-input-row">
   459→            <input
   460→              ref={inputRef}
   461→              id="share-pubkey-input"
   462→              type="text"
   463→              className={`share-input${error ? ' share-input--error' : ''}`}
   464→              placeholder="0x04..."
   465→              value={pubKeyInput}
   466→              onChange={(e) => {
   467→                setPubKeyInput(e.target.value);
   468→                setError(null);
   469→                setSuccess(null);
   470→              }}
   471→              onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
   472→              disabled={isSharing}
   473→              autoComplete="off"
   474→              spellCheck={false}
   475→            />
   476→            <button
   477→              type="button"
   478→              className="share-submit-btn"
   479→              onClick={handleShare}
   480→              disabled={isSharing || !pubKeyInput.trim()}
   481→            >
   482→              {isSharing ? '...' : '--share'}
   483→            </button>
   484→          </div>
   485→
   486→          {/* Error message */}
   487→          {error && (
   488→            <div className="share-error" role="alert">
   489→              {'> '}
   490→              {error}
   491→            </div>
   492→          )}
   493→
   494→          {/* Success message */}
   495→          {success && (
   496→            <div className="share-success" role="status">
   497→              {'> '}
   498→              {success}
   499→            </div>
   500→          )}
   501→
   502→          {/* Progress indicator for folder sharing */}
   503→          {progress && (
   504→            <div className="share-progress" role="status" aria-live="polite">
   505→              {'> '}re-wrapping keys... {progress.current}/{progress.total}
   506→              <div className="share-progress-bar">
   507→                <div
   508→                  className="share-progress-fill"
   509→                  style={{
   510→                    width:
   511→                      progress.total > 0 ? `${(progress.current / progress.total) * 100}%` : '0%',
   512→                  }}
   513→                />
   514→              </div>
   515→            </div>
   516→          )}
   517→        </div>
   518→
   519→        {/* Recipients section */}
   520→        <div className="share-recipients-section">
   521→          <div className="share-recipients-header">{'// recipients'}</div>
   522→
   523→          {recipientsLoading ? (
   524→            <div className="share-recipients-loading">loading...</div>
   525→          ) : recipients.length === 0 ? (
   526→            <div className="share-recipients-empty">no recipients yet</div>
   527→          ) : (
   528→            <div className="share-recipients-list">
   529→              {recipients.map((recipient) => (
   530→                <div key={recipient.shareId} className="share-recipient">
   531→                  <span className="share-recipient-key">
   532→                    {truncateKey(recipient.recipientPublicKey)}
   533→                  </span>
   534→
   535→                  {confirmRevokeId === recipient.shareId ? (
   536→                    <div className="share-revoke-confirm">
   537→                      <span className="share-revoke-confirm-text">{'confirm?'}</span>
   538→                      <button
   539→                        type="button"
   540→                        className="share-revoke-confirm-btn share-revoke-confirm-btn--yes"
   541→                        onClick={() => handleRevoke(recipient.shareId)}
   542→                        disabled={revokingId === recipient.shareId}
   543→                        aria-label="Confirm revoke"
   544→                      >
   545→                        {revokingId === recipient.shareId ? '...' : '[y]'}
   546→                      </button>
   547→                      <button
   548→                        type="button"
   549→                        className="share-revoke-confirm-btn share-revoke-confirm-btn--no"
   550→                        onClick={() => setConfirmRevokeId(null)}
   551→                        aria-label="Cancel revoke"
   552→                      >
   553→                        [n]
   554→                      </button>
   555→                    </div>
   556→                  ) : (
   557→                    <button
   558→                      type="button"
   559→                      className="share-revoke-btn"
   560→                      onClick={() => setConfirmRevokeId(recipient.shareId)}
   561→                      disabled={revokingId !== null}
   562→                      aria-label={`Revoke share for ${truncateKey(recipient.recipientPublicKey)}`}
   563→                    >
   564→                      --revoke
   565→                    </button>
   566→                  )}
   567→                </div>
   568→              ))}
   569→            </div>
   570→          )}
   571→        </div>
   572→      </div>
   573→    </Modal>
   574→  );
   575→}
   576→

     1→/**
     2→ * useSharedNavigation -- Navigation hook for browsing shared content.
     3→ *
     4→ * Similar to useFolderNavigation but with a different key source:
     5→ * - Top level: shares received via share records
     6→ * - Folder browsing: re-wrapped keys from share_keys table
     7→ * - File download: re-wrapped fileKey from share_keys table
     8→ *
     9→ * Security: All keys are ECIES-wrapped for the current user.
    10→ * The server never sees plaintext keys.
    11→ */
    12→
    13→import { useState, useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
    14→import {
    15→  unwrapKey,
    16→  hexToBytes,
    17→  decryptFolderMetadata,
    18→  decryptFileMetadata,
    19→  type FolderChild,
    20→  type FolderEntry,
    21→  type FilePointer,
    22→  type EncryptedFolderMetadata,
    23→  type EncryptedFileMetadata,
    24→} from '@cipherbox/crypto';
    25→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth.store';
    26→import { useShareStore, type ReceivedShare } from '../stores/share.store';
    27→import { fetchReceivedShares, fetchShareKeys, hideShare } from '../services/share.service';
    28→import { resolveIpnsRecord } from '../services/ipns.service';
    29→import { fetchFromIpfs } from '../lib/api/ipfs';
    30→import { downloadFile, triggerBrowserDownload } from '../services/download.service';
    31→import { useDownloadStore } from '../stores/download.store';
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * Breadcrumb entry for shared navigation.
    35→ */
    36→export type SharedBreadcrumb = {
    37→  id: string;
    38→  name: string;
    39→};
    40→
    41→/**
    42→ * A shared item displayed in the top-level shared list.
    43→ * Extends the original FolderChild with sharing metadata.
    44→ */
    45→export type SharedListItem = {
    46→  share: ReceivedShare;
    47→  /** Resolved folder children (for folders), or null (for files / unresolved) */
    48→  children: FolderChild[] | null;
    49→  /** Folder key for this shared item (decrypted from share record) */
    50→  folderKey: Uint8Array | null;
    51→};
    52→
    53→type SharedView = 'list' | 'folder';
    54→
    55→type UseSharedNavigationReturn = {
    56→  currentView: SharedView;
    57→  currentShareId: string | null;
    58→  sharedItems: SharedListItem[];
    59→  folderChildren: FolderChild[];
    60→  folderKey: Uint8Array | null;
    61→  breadcrumbs: SharedBreadcrumb[];
    62→  isLoading: boolean;
    63→  error: string | null;
    64→  navigateToShare: (shareId: string) => Promise<void>;
    65→  navigateToSubfolder: (folderId: string, folderName: string) => Promise<void>;
    66→  navigateUp: () => void;
    67→  navigateToRoot: () => void;
    68→  downloadSharedFile: (item: FilePointer) => Promise<void>;
    69→  hideSharedItem: (shareId: string) => Promise<void>;
    70→};
    71→
    72→/**
    73→ * Hook for browsing shared content.
    74→ *
    75→ * Manages the "Shared with me" browsing experience.
    76→ * Top-level view shows received shares as a flat list.
    77→ * Clicking a shared folder navigates into it using re-wrapped keys.
    78→ */
    79→export function useSharedNavigation(): UseSharedNavigationReturn {
    80→  const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState<SharedView>('list');
    81→  const [currentShareId, setCurrentShareId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    82→  const [sharedItems, setSharedItems] = useState<SharedListItem[]>([]);
    83→  const [folderChildren, setFolderChildren] = useState<FolderChild[]>([]);
    84→  const [folderKey, setFolderKey] = useState<Uint8Array | null>(null);
    85→  const [breadcrumbs, setBreadcrumbs] = useState<SharedBreadcrumb[]>([]);
    86→  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
    87→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    88→
    89→  // Navigation stack for folder browsing within a share
    90→  const navStackRef = useRef<
    91→    Array<{
    92→      folderId: string;
    93→      folderName: string;
    94→      children: FolderChild[];
    95→      folderKey: Uint8Array;
    96→    }>
    97→  >([]);
    98→
    99→  // Cache share keys per shareId with TTL to avoid refetching
   100→  const shareKeysCache = useRef<
   101→    Map<
   102→      string,
   103→      {
   104→        keys: Array<{ keyType: 'file' | 'folder'; itemId: string; encryptedKey: string }>;
   105→        fetchedAt: number;
   106→      }
   107→    >
   108→  >(new Map());
   109→
   110→  /**
   111→   * Load received shares on mount.
   112→   */
   113→  useEffect(() => {
   114→    let cancelled = false;
   115→
   116→    async function loadShares() {
   117→      setIsLoading(true);
   118→      setError(null);
   119→
   120→      try {
   121→        const shares = await fetchReceivedShares();
   122→        if (cancelled) return;
   123→
   124→        useShareStore.getState().setReceivedShares(shares);
   125→
   126→        const items: SharedListItem[] = shares.map((share) => ({
   127→          share,
   128→          children: null,
   129→          folderKey: null,
   130→        }));
   131→
   132→        setSharedItems(items);
   133→      } catch (err) {
   134→        if (cancelled) return;
   135→        console.error('Failed to load shared items:', err);
   136→        setError('Failed to load shared items');
   137→      } finally {
   138→        if (!cancelled) {
   139→          setIsLoading(false);
   140→        }
   141→      }
   142→    }
   143→
   144→    loadShares();
   145→    return () => {
   146→      cancelled = true;
   147→    };
   148→  }, []);
   149→
   150→  /** Cache TTL for share keys (60 seconds). */
   151→  const SHARE_KEYS_CACHE_TTL = 60_000;
   152→
   153→  /**
   154→   * Get share keys for a share, with TTL-based caching.
   155→   */
   156→  const getShareKeys = useCallback(async (shareId: string) => {
   157→    const cached = shareKeysCache.current.get(shareId);
   158→    if (cached && Date.now() - cached.fetchedAt < SHARE_KEYS_CACHE_TTL) {
   159→      return cached.keys;
   160→    }
   161→
   162→    const keys = await fetchShareKeys(shareId);
   163→    shareKeysCache.current.set(shareId, { keys, fetchedAt: Date.now() });
   164→    return keys;
   165→  }, []);
   166→
   167→  /**
   168→   * Navigate into a shared folder from the top-level list.
   169→   */
   170→  const navigateToShare = useCallback(
   171→    async (shareId: string) => {
   172→      const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   173→      if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
   174→        setError('No keypair available');
   175→        return;
   176→      }
   177→
   178→      const shareItem = sharedItems.find((s) => s.share.shareId === shareId);
   179→      if (!shareItem) return;
   180→
   181→      const share = shareItem.share;
   182→
   183→      setIsLoading(true);
   184→      setError(null);
   185→
   186→      try {
   187→        // Unwrap the shared item's key with our private key
   188→        const itemKey = await unwrapKey(
   189→          hexToBytes(share.encryptedKey),
   190→          auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey
   191→        );
   192→
   193→        if (share.itemType === 'folder') {
   194→          // Resolve folder IPNS to get metadata
   195→          const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(share.ipnsName);
   196→          if (!resolved) {
   197→            throw new Error('Could not resolve shared folder IPNS');
   198→          }
   199→
   200→          // Fetch and decrypt folder metadata
   201→          const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
   202→          const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
   203→          const encrypted: EncryptedFolderMetadata = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
   204→          const metadata = await decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, itemKey);
   205→
   206→          // Set folder state
   207→          setCurrentView('folder');
   208→          setCurrentShareId(shareId);
   209→          setFolderChildren(metadata.children ?? []);
   210→          setFolderKey(itemKey);
   211→          setBreadcrumbs([{ id: shareId, name: share.itemName }]);
   212→          navStackRef.current = [];
   213→        } else {
   214→          // For files, trigger download directly
   215→          await downloadSharedFileFromShare(share, auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey);
   216→        }
   217→      } catch (err) {
   218→        console.error('Failed to navigate to shared item:', err);
   219→        setError('Failed to open shared item');
   220→      } finally {
   221→        setIsLoading(false);
   222→      }
   223→    },
   224→    [sharedItems]
   225→  );
   226→
   227→  /**
   228→   * Navigate into a subfolder within a shared folder.
   229→   */
   230→  const navigateToSubfolder = useCallback(
   231→    async (folderId: string, folderName: string) => {
   232→      const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   233→      if (!auth.vaultKeypair || !currentShareId) return;
   234→
   235→      setIsLoading(true);
   236→      setError(null);
   237→
   238→      try {
   239→        // Get re-wrapped keys for this share
   240→        const keys = await getShareKeys(currentShareId);
   241→
   242→        // Find the re-wrapped folder key for this subfolder
   243→        const keyRecord = keys.find((k) => k.keyType === 'folder' && k.itemId === folderId);
   244→        if (!keyRecord) {
   245→          throw new Error('No key available for this subfolder');
   246→        }
   247→
   248→        // Unwrap the subfolder key
   249→        const subfolderKey = await unwrapKey(
   250→          hexToBytes(keyRecord.encryptedKey),
   251→          auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey
   252→        );
   253→
   254→        // Find the subfolder entry to get its IPNS name
   255→        const folderEntry = folderChildren.find(
   256→          (c): c is FolderEntry => c.type === 'folder' && c.id === folderId
   257→        );
   258→        if (!folderEntry) {
   259→          throw new Error('Subfolder not found in current children');
   260→        }
   261→
   262→        // Resolve subfolder IPNS
   263→        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(folderEntry.ipnsName);
   264→        if (!resolved) {
   265→          throw new Error('Could not resolve subfolder IPNS');
   266→        }
   267→
   268→        // Fetch and decrypt subfolder metadata
   269→        const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
   270→        const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
   271→        const encrypted: EncryptedFolderMetadata = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
   272→        const metadata = await decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, subfolderKey);
   273→
   274→        // Push current state to nav stack
   275→        if (folderKey) {
   276→          navStackRef.current.push({
   277→            folderId: breadcrumbs[breadcrumbs.length - 1]?.id ?? '',
   278→            folderName: breadcrumbs[breadcrumbs.length - 1]?.name ?? '',
   279→            children: folderChildren,
   280→            folderKey,
   281→          });
   282→        }
   283→
   284→        // Update state
   285→        setFolderChildren(metadata.children ?? []);
   286→        setFolderKey(subfolderKey);
   287→        setBreadcrumbs((prev) => [...prev, { id: folderId, name: folderName }]);
   288→      } catch (err) {
   289→        console.error('Failed to navigate to subfolder:', err);
   290→        setError('Failed to open subfolder');
   291→      } finally {
   292→        setIsLoading(false);
   293→      }
   294→    },
   295→    [currentShareId, folderChildren, folderKey, breadcrumbs, getShareKeys]
   296→  );
   297→
   298→  /**
   299→   * Navigate up one level.
   300→   */
   301→  const navigateUp = useCallback(() => {
   302→    if (navStackRef.current.length > 0) {
   303→      // Zero current folder key before replacing
   304→      if (folderKey) folderKey.fill(0);
   305→      // Pop from nav stack
   306→      const prev = navStackRef.current.pop()!;
   307→      setFolderChildren(prev.children);
   308→      setFolderKey(prev.folderKey);
   309→      setBreadcrumbs((crumbs) => crumbs.slice(0, -1));
   310→    } else if (currentView === 'folder') {
   311→      // Back to top-level list
   312→      navigateToRoot();
   313→    }
   314→  }, [currentView, folderKey]);
   315→
   316→  /**
   317→   * Navigate back to the top-level shared list.
   318→   * Zeroes all decrypted folder keys from memory before clearing state.
   319→   */
   320→  const navigateToRoot = useCallback(() => {
   321→    // Zero current folder key
   322→    if (folderKey) folderKey.fill(0);
   323→    // Zero all nav stack folder keys
   324→    for (const entry of navStackRef.current) {
   325→      entry.folderKey.fill(0);
   326→    }
   327→    setCurrentView('list');
   328→    setCurrentShareId(null);
   329→    setFolderChildren([]);
   330→    setFolderKey(null);
   331→    setBreadcrumbs([]);
   332→    navStackRef.current = [];
   333→    setError(null);
   334→  }, [folderKey]);
   335→
   336→  /**
   337→   * Download a shared file from the top-level list.
   338→   * The share's encryptedKey wraps the parent folder key (needed to decrypt file metadata).
   339→   * The actual file key is stored as a child key in share_keys.
   340→   */
   341→  async function downloadSharedFileFromShare(
   342→    share: ReceivedShare,
   343→    privateKey: Uint8Array
   344→  ): Promise<void> {
   345→    if (share.itemType !== 'file') return;
   346→
   347→    const downloadStore = useDownloadStore.getState();
   348→
   349→    try {
   350→      downloadStore.startDownload(share.itemName);
   351→
   352→      // Unwrap the parent folder key from the share record
   353→      const parentFolderKey = await unwrapKey(hexToBytes(share.encryptedKey), privateKey);
   354→
   355→      // Resolve file IPNS metadata and decrypt with parent folder key
   356→      const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(share.ipnsName);
   357→      if (!resolved) {
   358→        throw new Error('File metadata IPNS not found');
   359→      }
   360→
   361→      const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
   362→      const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
   363→      const encrypted: EncryptedFileMetadata = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
   364→      const fileMeta = await decryptFileMetadata(encrypted, parentFolderKey);
   365→      parentFolderKey.fill(0);
   366→
   367→      // Get the re-wrapped file key from share_keys
   368→      const keys = await fetchShareKeys(share.shareId);
   369→      const fileKeyRecord = keys.find((k) => k.keyType === 'file');
   370→      if (!fileKeyRecord) {
   371→        throw new Error('No re-wrapped file key available for this file');
   372→      }
   373→
   374→      // Download and decrypt using the re-wrapped file key
   375→      const plaintext = await downloadFile(
   376→        {
   377→          cid: fileMeta.cid,
   378→          iv: fileMeta.fileIv,
   379→          wrappedKey: fileKeyRecord.encryptedKey,
   380→          originalName: share.itemName,
   381→          encryptionMode: fileMeta.encryptionMode,
   382→        },
   383→        privateKey
   384→      );
   385→
   386→      downloadStore.setDecrypting();
   387→      triggerBrowserDownload(plaintext, share.itemName);
   388→      downloadStore.setSuccess();
   389→    } catch (err) {
   390→      const message = (err as Error).message || 'Download failed';
   391→      downloadStore.setError(message);
   392→      console.error('Shared file download failed:', err);
   393→    }
   394→  }
   395→
   396→  /**
   397→   * Download a shared file from within a shared folder.
   398→   * Uses re-wrapped file keys from share_keys.
   399→   */
   400→  const downloadSharedFile = useCallback(
   401→    async (item: FilePointer) => {
   402→      const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   403→      if (!auth.vaultKeypair || !currentShareId || !folderKey) return;
   404→
   405→      const downloadStore = useDownloadStore.getState();
   406→
   407→      try {
   408→        downloadStore.startDownload(item.name);
   409→
   410→        // Get share keys for file key lookup
   411→        const keys = await getShareKeys(currentShareId);
   412→
   413→        // First resolve the file metadata using the parent folder key
   414→        const resolved = await resolveIpnsRecord(item.fileMetaIpnsName);
   415→        if (!resolved) {
   416→          throw new Error('File metadata IPNS not found');
   417→        }
   418→
   419→        const encryptedBytes = await fetchFromIpfs(resolved.cid);
   420→        const encryptedJson = new TextDecoder().decode(encryptedBytes);
   421→        const encrypted: EncryptedFileMetadata = JSON.parse(encryptedJson);
   422→        const fileMeta = await decryptFileMetadata(encrypted, folderKey);
   423→
   424→        // Look for a re-wrapped file key in share_keys
   425→        const fileKeyRecord = keys.find((k) => k.keyType === 'file' && k.itemId === item.id);
   426→
   427→        if (!fileKeyRecord) {
   428→          throw new Error('No re-wrapped file key available for this file');
   429→        }
   430→
   431→        // Use re-wrapped file key from share_keys
   432→        // downloadFile handles unwrapping internally via wrappedKey + privateKey
   433→        const plaintext = await downloadFile(
   434→          {
   435→            cid: fileMeta.cid,
   436→            iv: fileMeta.fileIv,
   437→            wrappedKey: fileKeyRecord.encryptedKey,
   438→            originalName: item.name,
   439→            encryptionMode: fileMeta.encryptionMode,
   440→          },
   441→          auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey
   442→        );
   443→
   444→        downloadStore.setDecrypting();
   445→        triggerBrowserDownload(plaintext, item.name);
   446→        downloadStore.setSuccess();
   447→      } catch (err) {
   448→        const message = (err as Error).message || 'Download failed';
   449→        downloadStore.setError(message);
   450→        console.error('Shared file download failed:', err);
   451→      }
   452→    },
   453→    [currentShareId, folderKey, getShareKeys]
   454→  );
   455→
   456→  /**
   457→   * Hide a shared item from the user's view.
   458→   */
   459→  const hideSharedItem = useCallback(async (shareId: string) => {
   460→    try {
   461→      await hideShare(shareId);
   462→      useShareStore.getState().removeReceivedShare(shareId);
   463→      setSharedItems((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.share.shareId !== shareId));
   464→    } catch (err) {
   465→      console.error('Failed to hide share:', err);
   466→      setError('Failed to hide shared item');
   467→    }
   468→  }, []);
   469→
   470→  return {
   471→    currentView,
   472→    currentShareId,
   473→    sharedItems,
   474→    folderChildren,
   475→    folderKey,
   476→    breadcrumbs,
   477→    isLoading,
   478→    error,
   479→    navigateToShare,
   480→    navigateToSubfolder,
   481→    navigateUp,
   482→    navigateToRoot,
   483→    downloadSharedFile,
   484→    hideSharedItem,
   485→  };
   486→}
   487→
